### [91] [A Scaling Framework for Mechanical Memristance: Dimensionless Metrics and Material Design Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18364)
*Abdulla Alhembar,Fabrizio Scarpa,Chrystel D. L. Remillat,Rodrigo J. da Silva,Ross Anderson,Ludovico Cademartiri,Abderrezak Bezazi,James P. K. Armstrong,Adam W. Perriman*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出分数阶力学忆阻器模型并无量纲化，定义有效忆阻度筛选指数，用于统一比较多种材料的记忆依赖阻尼。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 力学忆阻器的耗散响应依赖加载历史与演化内态，此类行为广泛存在于黏弹聚合物、形状记忆材料、压电体、颗粒介质及场响应流体中；但需本构判据与尺度比较才能确定哪些响应可纳入力学忆阻器表述。

Method: 构建分数阶力学忆阻器模型，改写为无量纲形式，识别控制记忆依赖耗散的主导参数，导出表征耗散幅值、记忆态尺度与记忆传递的无量纲群，并组合为有效力学忆阻度筛选指数 Mh = βγ|H_α(Ω)|。

Result: 该指数在匹配响应幅值、本构斜率与参考尺度下给出局部阻尼调制的条件性度量；据此对形状记忆聚合物、形状记忆合金、水凝胶、纳米纤维素、富木质素材料、天然纤维、压电聚合物与陶瓷、电流变液、磁流变液及颗粒阻尼器等材料类别构建了示意性参数情景。

Conclusion: 该框架在所采用的本构描述下为比较记忆依赖阻尼提供共同基准，并明确了将其应用于候选材料与器件所需的标定条件。

Abstract: Mechanical memristors are systems whose dissipative response depends on the history of previous loading through an evolving internal state. History-dependent forces and dissipation occur in a wide range of materials and devices, including viscoelastic polymers, shape-memory materials, piezoelectrics, granular media and field-responsive fluids. Determining which of these responses admits a mechanical-memristor representation requires a constitutive test, as well as a comparison of scales. In this work, a fractional-order mechanical memristor model is developed and cast into a nondimensional form to identify the governing parameters controlling memory-dependent dissipation. The formulation leads to a set of dimensionless groups that characterise dissipation magnitude, memory-state scale and memory transfer. These quantities are combined into an effective mechanical memristance screening index \(\Mh = βγ|\mathcal H_α(Ω)|\), which provides a conditional measure of local damping modulation at matched response amplitude, constitutive slope and reference scales. Illustrative parameter scenarios are then constructed for material classes including shape-memory polymers, shape-memory alloys, hydrogels, nanocellulose, lignin-rich materials, natural fibres, piezoelectric polymers, piezoelectric ceramics, electrorheological fluids, magnetorheological fluids and granular dampers. The framework establishes a common basis for comparing memory-dependent damping within the adopted constitutive description and identifies the calibration required for its application to candidate materials and devices

</details>

### [92] [Harmonic Vector Fields and Betti Numbers in Bounded Three-Dimensional Electromagnetic Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18076)
*Wei Jiang,Jie Liu*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 本文用矢量分析、环量积分、切割面、标量Laplace问题、Stokes定理与Green恒等式，对拓扑复杂有界三维区域中的调和矢量场进行几何化、构造性研究，并通过第一、第二Betti数分别解释"独立手柄型环量"与"包围空洞"，给出Neumann型与Dirichlet型调和场空间维数的直接证明，并推广至各向异性无损介质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有界三维区域的拓扑会显著影响电磁场：在拓扑复杂区域中，无旋场可能不存在全局单值标量势，无散场也可能不存在全局矢量势。这些拓扑效应自然引出Helmholtz分解中的调和矢量场。因此需要一种几何化、构造性的方法来刻画这些场，并给出谐调场维度与物理直流模式（DC modes）的清晰拓扑解释，以支撑有界电磁谐振器等应用中的场分析。

Method: 方法基于经典矢量分析工具：环量积分、切割面（cutting surfaces）、标量Laplace问题、Stokes定理与Green恒等式。用第一Betti数（独立手柄型环量）和第二Betti数（包围空洞）刻画区域拓扑；对Neumann型与Dirichlet型调和场空间维数给出直接证明；并将分析框架推广到各向异性无损介质。

Result: 证明了Neumann型与Dirichlet型调和场空间的维数，并给出其与区域拓扑（第一、第二Betti数）的对应关系：第一Betti数对应独立手柄型环量，第二Betti数对应包围空洞。分析可推广至各向异性无损媒质。由此得到调和场及有界电磁谐振器中物理DC模式的简明拓扑解释。

Conclusion: 拓扑复杂的有界三维区域中，调和矢量场可由拓扑不变量（第一、第二Betti数）几何化地刻画：它们分别体现独立手柄型环量与包围空洞的效应。该框架为Helmholtz分解中的调和场及电磁谐振器DC模式提供了简洁的拓扑解释，并可扩展至各向异性无损介质。

Abstract: The topology of a bounded three-dimensional domain can strongly affect electromagnetic fields. In a topologically complex domain, a curl-free field may not have a globally single-valued scalar potential and a divergence-free field may also fail to have a global vector potential. These topological effects lead naturally to harmonic vector fields in the Helmholtz decomposition. This paper gives a geometric and constructive study of such fields using vector analysis, circulation integrals, cutting surfaces, scalar Laplace problems, Stokes' theorem, and Green's identity. The first Betti number is interpreted through independent handle-type circulations, whereas the second Betti number counts enclosed voids. Direct proofs are given for the dimensions of the Neumann and Dirichlet harmonic-field spaces. The analysis is also extended to anisotropic lossless media. The results provide a simple topological interpretation of harmonic fields and physical DC modes in bounded electromagnetic resonators.

</details>


### [93] [Impact of Phase Unwrapping on Multitarget Acoustic Lenses for Transcranial Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18495)
*D. Attali,T. Tiennot,M. Tanter,Jean-Francois Aubry*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 本研究提出双焦点声学透镜，用于补偿人颅骨像差并同时靶向脑深部多结构，重点考察相位解缠对透镜设计的优化作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经颅超声深脑刺激中，声波穿越人颅骨会产生相位畸变，需借助声学透镜补偿；同时靶向多个深部结构对透镜设计提出更高要求。

Method: 设计双焦点透镜，研究相位解缠对透镜设计的影响，并在N=5个人颅骨样本、两种目标空间排布条件下评估压力场分布。

Result: 对设计中所计算相位进行解缠可提升跨颅骨压力场的保真度；空间精度平均提高73%，靶外能量沉积平均降低58%。

Conclusion: 相位解缠对优化未来多区域经颅超声刺激的安全性与有效性具有重要意义。

Abstract: Acoustic lenses have been introduced recently to compensate for the phase distortions induced by the propagation across a human skull for ultrasonic deep-brain stimulation in humans. In this study, we present bifocal lenses that compensate for human skull aberrations and allow simultaneous targeting of multiple structures deep in the brain. We investigated the impact of phase unwrapping in the design of the lenses and how this process improves the distribution of pressure produced in N=5 human skulls for two different spatial arrangements of the targets. The results show that unwrapping the phase computed during the design increases the fidelity of the pressure field generated across the human skulls. The spatial precision is on average improved by 73%, and out of target energy deposition is on average reduced by 58%. The results presented in this study highlight the importance of phase unwrapping to optimize the safety and efficacy of future transcranial ultrasound stimulations targeting multiple regions.

</details>


<div id='eess.AS'></div>

### [94] [Encoder Awakening via Adapters: Effective Domain-Adaptive Fine-tuning of Speech-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17981)
*Mohan Shi,Zilai Wang,Natarajan Balaji Shankar,Kaiyuan Zhang,Eray Eren,Abeer Alwan*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 该论文提出EAVA方法，通过适配器唤醒语音编码器，提升Speech-LLM在域偏移语音（儿童、方言）上的ASR性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Speech-LLM在通用领域ASR表现优异，但在目标域数据有限的域偏移场景（如儿童或方言语音）下适应困难。由于交叉熵损失仅在LLM输出端计算，LLM占主导地位，导致语音编码器对新声学条件适应不足。

Method: 提出Encoder Awakening via Adapters (EAVA)：首先在编码器每层插入轻量级适配器并仅训练适配器，将目标域声学知识注入编码器，同时保留预训练知识；然后对整个模型在目标域进行联合微调，并对LLM应用LoRA。

Result: 在三个域偏移ASR数据集（涵盖儿童和方言语音）上，EAVA持续优于原始微调及其他基线，达到新的最先进水平。

Conclusion: EAVA是一种简单有效的域自适应微调方法，通过唤醒语音编码器解决了Speech-LLM在域偏移语音上的适应不足问题。

Abstract: Speech Large Language Models (Speech-LLMs), typically built from a pre-trained speech encoder, a modality projector, and an LLM fine-tuned with Low-Rank Adapters (LoRA), have shown strong Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) performance on general-domain speech. However, adapting them to domain-shifted speech, such as child or dialectal speech, remains challenging under limited target-domain data. Given the dominant role of the LLM in Speech-LLMs, with cross-entropy loss applied only at the LLM output, the speech encoder may receive insufficient adaptation to new acoustic conditions. In this paper, we propose Encoder Awakening via Adapters (EAVA), a simple yet effective domain-adaptive fine-tuning method for Speech-LLM-based ASR. First, lightweight adapters are inserted into each encoder layer and trained exclusively, enabling target-domain acoustic knowledge to be incorporated into the encoder while preserving its pre-trained knowledge. Second, the full model is jointly fine-tuned on the target domain with LoRA applied to the LLM. Experiments on three domain-shifted ASR datasets, covering child and dialectal speech, show that EAVA consistently outperforms vanilla fine-tuning and other baselines, achieving new state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [95] [G-Mamba: Sparse Graph-Guided Mamba for Audio-Visual Speech Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18009)
*Guo-Ruei Tseng,Hung-Shin Lee,Hsin-Min Wang,Berlin Chen*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出稀疏图引导Mamba（SG-Mamba）轻量级音视频语音增强框架，用稀疏异构图建模跨模态关系、Mamba捕获长时上下文，在LRS3噪声条件下达13.091 dB SI-SDR，开销仅3.45 G MACs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量级音视频语音增强（AVSE）存在计算效率与跨模态对齐精度的权衡：简单拼接缺乏关系表达能力，而稠密交叉注意力计算开销大，且在强声学干扰下跨模态对应关系不可靠。

Method: SG-Mamba将稀疏异构图与线性复杂度的Mamba主干相结合。图通过内容自适应注意力与跨帧音视频连接显式建模模态特定关系，Mamba捕获长时时间上下文；并引入音频跳跃连接以在噪声抑制的同时保留频谱细节。

Result: 在LRS3上，SG-Mamba对比强轻量级基线取得相当或更优性能，纯噪声条件下SI-SDR达13.091 dB；在复杂多说话人条件下保持鲁棒，计算开销为3.45 G MACs（6.90 G FLOPs）。在VoxCeleb2上的结果进一步表明显式结构化先验可提升轻量级AVSE的鲁棒性、泛化性和计算效率。

Conclusion: 显式结构先验（稀疏异构图）与线性复杂度序列建模（Mamba）的结合，可在低计算代价下有效改善轻量级AVSE的跨模态对齐与鲁棒性，是一种兼顾效率与性能的可行方案。

Abstract: Lightweight audio-visual speech enhancement (AVSE) models face a critical trade-off between computational efficiency and cross-modal alignment accuracy. While simple concatenation lacks relational expressiveness, dense cross-attention incurs computational overhead and is prone to unreliable cross-modal correspondence under strong acoustic interference. We propose Sparse Graph-Guided Mamba (SG-Mamba), a lightweight AVSE framework that integrates a sparse heterogeneous graph with a linear-complexity Mamba backbone. The graph explicitly models modality-specific relations through content-adaptive attention and cross-frame audio-visual connections, while Mamba captures long-range temporal context. We further introduce an audio skip connection to preserve spectral detail without sacrificing noise suppression. Evaluated on LRS3, SG-Mamba achieves competitive or superior performance against strong lightweight baselines and reaches 13.091 dB SI-SDR under noise-only condition. It also remains robust in cluttered multi-speaker conditions with a competitive cost of 3.45 G MACs (or 6.90 G FLOPs). Results on VoxCeleb2 further suggest that explicit structural priors improve robustness, generalizability, and computational efficiency in lightweight AVSE.

</details>


### [96] [Task-oriented neural FOA encoding for SELD from irregular microphone arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18040)
*Jiachen Liu,Yin Cao,Ming Wu,Jun Yang*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出两阶段SELD框架，从麦克风阵列信号学习任务导向、兼容FOA的表示，通过残差编码与教师-学生蒸馏提升定位检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SELD系统通常依赖一阶Ambisonics（FOA）输入，但从非规则麦克风阵列获取有效FOA表示仍具挑战性。作者希望学习任务导向的FOA兼容表示以替代传统编码。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段用神经残差编码器，通过信号相关校正精炼传统FOA编码；第二阶段采用教师-学生方案，通过帧级排列不变知识蒸馏，将事件与空间知识从理论FOA表示迁移至学生模型。实验在合成场景（四面体与12通道Benchmark阵列）及LOCATA真实静止声源录音上进行。进行信号级分析考察FOA重建误差与SELD性能关系。

Result: 教师引导持续提升下游SELD性能，并显著降低定位误差。信号级分析显示，较低的FOA重建误差并不必然带来更好的SELD性能。

Conclusion: 蒸馏表示主要针对任务相关空间信息进行优化，而非严格FOA重建，说明任务导向表示学习对非规则阵列SELD具有价值。

Abstract: Sound event localization and detection (SELD) systems often rely on first-order Ambisonics (FOA) input, whereas obtaining useful FOA representations from irregular microphone arrays remains challenging. This paper proposes a two-stage SELD framework that learns a task-oriented, FOA-compatible representation from microphone-array signals. A neural residual encoder first refines conventional FOA encoding through a signal-dependent correction. A teacher--student scheme then transfers event and spatial knowledge from theoretical FOA representations through frame-level permutation-invariant knowledge distillation. Experiments on synthetic scenes with tetrahedral and 12-channel Benchmark arrays, together with real stationary-source recordings from the LOCATA dataset, show that teacher guidance consistently improves downstream SELD performance and substantially reduces localization error. Signal-level analysis further shows that lower FOA reconstruction error does not necessarily correspond to better SELD performance, indicating that the distilled representation is optimized primarily for task-relevant spatial information rather than strict FOA reconstruction.

</details>


### [97] [Correlation-Guided Encoder Selection for Multi-Encoder Large Audio-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18041)
*Pei-Jun Liao,Hung-Shin Lee,Wenze Ren,Kuo-Hsuan Hung,Hung-yi Lee,Hsin-Min Wang*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出CUES方法，仅用单编码器评估的任务/类别级Pearson相关性估计互补性来选择编码器融合集合，无需融合训练。在XARES-LLM上，Track A选出跨家族三编码器组合取得4.3%相对增益，Track B选出语音专注双编码器组合取得6.3%相对增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多编码器融合可扩展大音频语言模型（LALMs）的能力，但凭直觉或穷举选择编码器会引入冗余表示并加剧本就受限的算力预算。因此需要一种低成本、能有效识别编码器互补性的选择方法。

Method: CUES（Correlation-gUided Encoder Selection）：通过编码器性能剖面之间的任务级与类别级Pearson相关性来估计互补性，仅基于单编码器评估即可为候选集合打分，选择过程中不进行融合训练。在XARES-LLM基准上使用冻结的SmolLM2-135M骨干（LoRA适配）进行五折交叉验证，仅使用留出开发集选择配置，不使用测试数据。

Result: CUES在各track上从留出开发集稳定选出同一配置。Track A广谱套件选择跨家族三编码器组合（Whisper-medium、mHuBERT-147、Dasheng-base），相对Whisper-medium提升4.3%（0.771 vs. 0.739）。Track B文本生成选择聚焦的纯语音双编码器（mHuBERT-147与WavLM-base-plus），并主动放弃加入发散性编码器，相对mHuBERT-147提升6.3%（0.589 vs. 0.554）。

Conclusion: 该分歧并非扩展失败，而是符合多样性—干扰权衡：CUES仅凭相关性信号即可按track导航。在所评估的编码器池中，加入跨家族多样性在广谱音频任务上呈倒U型趋势，而在文本生成上则持续退化，后者更偏好聚焦、以语音为锚的编码器集合。

Abstract: Multi-encoder fusion extends Large Audio-Language Models (LALMs) beyond speech-centric recognition, but selecting encoders via intuition or exhaustive search often introduces redundant representations and inflates an already constrained compute budget. We propose CUES (Correlation-gUided Encoder Selection), a lightweight heuristic that estimates complementarity through task- and category-level Pearson correlations between encoders' performance profiles, scoring a candidate set from single-encoder evaluations alone--without fusion training during selection. Evaluated on the XARES-LLM benchmark with a frozen SmolLM2-135M backbone (LoRA-adapted) via five-fold cross-validation, CUES consistently identifies the same configuration per track from held-out development splits alone, without using test data for selection. For the broad Track~A suite, CUES selects a cross-family trio (Whisper-medium, mHuBERT-147, and Dasheng-base), achieving a 4.3% relative gain over Whisper-medium (0.771 vs. 0.739). For Track~B text generation, it re-anchors on a focused, speech-only pair (mHuBERT-147 and WavLM-base-plus) and actively abstains from adding a divergent encoder, outperforming mHuBERT-147 by 6.3% (0.589 vs. 0.554). Rather than a failure to scale, this divergence is consistent with a diversity--interference trade-off that CUES navigates per track from correlation signals alone: across the evaluated pool, added cross-family diversity tends toward an inverted-U on broad audio tasks but toward steady degradation on text generation, which favors a focused, speech-anchored set.

</details>


### [98] [Reviving Etter method for autoregressive inpainting: Generalization, evaluation, implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18401)
*Ondřej Mokrý,Matěj Hrdlička,Pavel Rajmic*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文复现并扩展了Etter提出的自回归(AR)音频插值方法，提供开源实现，并提出高阶级数扩展与因果化变体，在80 ms以内缺损的音乐片段上达到多数AR与稀疏类基线方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频波形中的缺损（如掉线与丢包）需要修复；Etter的AR插值方法虽结合前向与后向预测并构建结构化线性系统，但缺乏面向音频信号的公开完整实现，故本文补足实现并提升其适用性。

Method: 复现Etter的AR插值（前向/后向AR预测联立的线性系统），提供开源实现；提出两项扩展：(1) 允许高阶AR模型在缺损长度短于模型阶数时仍可求解的表述；(2) 面向丢包隐藏的因果变体。评测使用音乐片段，缺损长度至多80 ms，指标为SNR与感知动机的客观差异等级(ODG)，并辅以听音测试。

Result: Etter插值性能与大多数AR类和稀疏类基线方法相当，但不及迭代的、逐缺损(gap-wise)处理的Janssen方法。

Conclusion: Etter的AR插值经开源实现与两项实用扩展后，是一种有效且具竞争力的音频插值方案，可用于非因果修复与因果丢包隐藏，但在长缺损下仍逊于Janssen的迭代方法。

Abstract: Audio inpainting aims to restore missing segments in an audio waveform, as encountered in dropouts and packet losses. This paper revisits the autoregressive (AR) interpolation method proposed by Etter, which combines forward and backward AR prediction through a structured linear system, yet lacks a widely used full implementation for audio signals. We provide an open implementation and propose two practical extensions: A formulation that allows high-order AR models even when the gap length is shorter than the model order, and a causal variant tailored to packet loss concealment. The method is evaluated on musical excerpts with gaps up to 80 ms, using SNR and perceptually motivated objective difference grades, complemented by a listening test. The results show that Etter inpainting matches the performance of most AR- and sparsity-based baseline methods, with the exception of the iterative, gap-wise Janssen approach.

</details>


### [99] [Mask-Based Speech Enhancement for Spatial Audio: A Comparison of Ambisonics, Beamforming, and Microphone Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18532)
*Sheli Hendel,Boaz Rafaely,Dorothea Kolossa*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了时频掩蔽在三种信号表示（麦克风信号、波束形成器输出、Ambisonics信号）上的表现，揭示语音增强与空间保真度之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于掩蔽的语音增强广泛用于抑制噪声与干扰，但其在多通道输出空间音频算法中的性能尚缺乏系统研究；此类场景需在提升语音质量的同时保留定位、空间感知和空间释放掩蔽所需的空间线索。

Method: 将时频掩蔽分别应用于三种信号表示：麦克风信号、波束形成器输出和Ambisonics信号，并从语音质量、可懂度、双耳线索保持和混响保持四个维度进行评估。

Result: 结果显示出增强与空间保真度之间存在明显权衡：波束形成器域掩蔽取得最高的语音增强评分，而Ambisonics域掩蔽更好地保留了残余干扰的空间属性；所有方法均能保持目标的定位线索。

Conclusion: 在多通道空间音频场景中，掩蔽域的选择需权衡增强效果与空间保真度：追求增强性能可选波束形成器域，追求空间属性保持可选Ambisonics域，且各方法均不损害目标定位线索。

Abstract: Mask-based speech enhancement is widely used for suppressing noise and interference, but its performance in spatial audio algorithms with multichannel output has not been studied extensively. In such settings, speech enhancement must improve speech quality while preserving spatial cues that are essential for localization, spatial awareness, and spatial release from masking. In this work, we systematically compare time frequency masking applied to three signal representations: microphone signals, beamformer outputs, and Ambisonics signals. Performance is evaluated in terms of speech quality, intelligibility, binaural cue preservation, and reverberation preservation. Results reveal a clear trade-off between enhancement and spatial fidelity: beamformer-domain masking achieves the highest speech enhancement scores, while Ambisonics-domain masking better preserves the spatial attributes of the residual interference. All methods preserve the target's localization cues.

</details>


### [100] [Absolute Quality Ratings of Speech Enhancement Systems by Listeners of Different Ages and Degrees of Hearing Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18714)
*Matteo Torcoli,Chih-Wei Wu,Andrea Esposito,Phillip A. Williams,Katrien Cambier,William Wolcott,Antonio Curci,Nicholas S. Reed,Mark Laureyns*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 该研究比较了年轻正常听力者与老年（含不同听力状况）听者对语音增强系统语音质量的主观评分，发现老年组对系统间差异的区分度显著收缩，且听力损失程度不能完全解释这一现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音增强（SE）旨在改善聆听体验，尤其面向老年及年龄相关性听力损失人群；然而语音质量（SQ）评估通常由年轻正常听力听者完成，其评分能否推广至老年群体尚不明确。

Method: 经筛选后，招募40名年轻正常听力听者（20–30岁）与67名老年听者（60–95岁，听力图特征多样）；测试材料为带真实背景声的自然对话；比较两组对各SE系统的绝对SQ评分。

Result: 年轻听者能清晰区分的SE系统间SQ差异，在老年组中变小甚至无法区分，且不受听力状态影响；听力损失严重程度与绝对评分降低相关，但未显著调节可区分差异的收缩；一小部分听力图混合的老年听者呈现类似年轻听者的评分模式。

Conclusion: 以年轻正常听力听者获得的SQ评分难以直接外推至老年人群；外周听力学指标不足以解释评分区分的收缩，提示存在其他（如中枢或认知）因素，SE评估需纳入多样化老年听者。

Abstract: Speech Enhancement (SE) supports listening, particularly for older adults with age-related hearing loss. Yet, enhanced Speech Quality (SQ) is commonly evaluated by young normal-hearing listeners, and how their ratings translate to older adults remains under-explored. We compared absolute SQ ratings from 40 younger normal-hearing listeners (20-30 years) and 67 older listeners (60-95 years) with diverse audiometric profiles, after screening. Test materials comprised natural dialogues with realistic backgrounds. SQ differences between SE systems that were clear for younger listeners were smaller or inseparable in older groups, regardless of hearing status. Hearing loss severity was associated with lower absolute ratings, but did not strongly modulate the contraction in separable SQ differences. A small, audiometrically mixed subgroup of older listeners showed younger-like rating patterns, suggesting that peripheral audiology alone cannot explain the contraction.

</details>


### [101] [GrainSpeech: Less Context, More Detail for Compact Speech Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18856)
*Zitao Liang,Chang Gao*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 紧凑声学模型存在质量-容量权衡。本文研究编码器上下文与Mel谱监督两个因素，提出固定感受野卷积编码器与Mel专用梯度方差监督，仅264.8K参数即实现MCU上17.9倍实时Mel生成，且UTMOS可与参数量大得多的模型媲美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑声学模型需在质量与容量间取得平衡，但编码器上下文长度应取多大、以及如何对Mel谱进行有效监督尚不清晰。作者希望明确感受野大小的影响，并设计适合Mel域的监督方式以在极小参数下保持合成质量。

Method: 1) 感受野缩放研究：逐步扩大自注意力范围，观察对音高、能量、时长预测的影响；2) 据此设计固定感受野的卷积编码器；3) 监督方面，先尝试直接迁移图像域的梯度方差监督，发现其可恢复细尺度变化但损害预测质量，进而提出Mel专用方案：轴特定梯度、重叠局部统计、对数域方差匹配。

Result: 感受野缩放实验表明，自注意力超过15个音素后，对音高、能量、时长预测无一致增益。固定感受野卷积编码器将三项预测误差分别降低36.0%、17.3%、3.4%。GrainSpeech仅264.8K参数，在MCU上达到17.9倍实时Mel生成，UTMOS与参数量远超其（超1.5%）的模型相当。

Conclusion: 在紧凑声学模型中，超出约15音素的自注意力扩展收益有限，固定感受野卷积编码器更有效；Mel专用梯度方差监督优于直接迁移图像域方法。该方案以极小参数量在MCU上实现高质量、高速Mel生成，代码与演示已开源。

Abstract: Compact acoustic models face a challenging quality-capacity trade-off. We investigate two factors in this regime: encoder context and Mel-spectrogram supervision. A receptive-field-scaling study shows that expanding self-attention beyond 15 phonemes provides no consistent gains in pitch, energy, or duration prediction. Guided by this finding, we introduce a fixed-receptive-field convolutional encoder that reduces the respective prediction errors by 36.0%, 17.3%, and 3.4%. We further show that directly transferring image-domain gradient-variance supervision restores fine-scale variation but degrades predicted quality, motivating a Mel-specific formulation with axis-specific gradients, overlapping local statistics, and log-domain variance matching. GrainSpeech contains only 264.8K parameters and achieves 17.9x real-time Mel generation on a microcontroller (MCU), while attaining UTMOS scores comparable to substantially larger models with less than 1.5% of their parameters. Source code and demos are available at https://github.com/lab-emi/GrainSpeech.

</details>


<div id='nlin.SI'></div>

# nlin.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [Maxwell's relations as Hamilton's equations: a symplectic and variational framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18215)
*Sikarin Yoo-Kong*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 将经典热力学重构为哈密顿动力系统，通过正则映射把麦克斯韦关系、热力学约束与态函数路径无关性统一解释为几何/多时可积性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热力学中麦克斯韦关系、状态函数路径无关性等通常作为独立公设或经验结论引入，缺乏与哈密顿力学同等统一的几何表述；作者希望用哈密顿动力学与变分/多时几何框架给出统一推导。

Method: 建立正则映射 (q,p,t,H)↔(V,−P,S,−T)，把热力学势视为作用量泛函，构造热力学 Poincaré–Cartan 一形式并用其特征方程对应麦克斯韦关系；对理想气体绝热过程导出 V(T)、P(T) 的二阶常微分方程及温度依赖拉格朗日量；将各分量拉格朗日量组装为多参数拉格朗日 1-形式 L 并检验闭条件 dL=0。

Result: 在该几何框架下，麦克斯韦关系被识别为热力学 Poincaré–Cartan 一形式的特征方程；变分原理同时恢复麦克斯韦关系与绝热性、等温性等守恒第一积分；理想气体绝热过程可由显式二阶常微分方程描述；并在解流形上证明 dL=0 成立。

Conclusion: 热力学状态函数的路径无关性并非独立公设，而是多时可积性在拉格朗日 1-形式闭条件下的几何推论；该框架将热力学结构与哈密顿动力学形式统一起来。

Abstract: We develop a geometric framework in which classical thermodynamics is reformulated as a Hamiltonian dynamical system. An explicit canonical mapping $(q,p,t,H)\leftrightarrow(V,-P,S,-T)$ identifies Maxwell's relations as the characteristic equations of the thermodynamic Poincaré--Cartan one-form, in direct parallel with Hamilton's equations of motion. Treating thermodynamic potentials as action functionals, a variational principle recovers both the Maxwell relations and the thermodynamic constraints (adiabaticity, isothermality) as conserved first integrals. For adiabatic processes in an ideal gas, this yields explicit second-order ordinary differential equations for $V(T)$ and $P(T)$, each governed by a temperature-dependent Lagrangian. Assembling the component Lagrangians into a multi-parameter Lagrangian 1-form $\mathcal{L}$, we prove that the closure condition $d\mathcal{L}=0$ holds on the solution manifold, establishing that path-independence of thermodynamic state functions is a geometric consequence of multi-time integrability rather than an independent postulate.

</details>


<div id='cs.SD'></div>

# cs.SD [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Timbre Analysis of the Hulusi, a Southwestern Chinese Free-Reed Instrument, using Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17612)
*Yang Xia,Rolf Bader*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文使用COMSAR框架下的机器学习模型（Kohonen自组织映射）对葫芦丝的音色特征进行聚类分析，发现分形相关维数是最佳聚类特征，高音区的混沌性最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 葫芦丝近期极为流行，但其音色特性尚缺乏系统研究；同时该乐器以管长而非自由簧本征频率决定音高，与传统西方自由簧乐器（如手风琴、布鲁斯口琴）不同，具有独特的研究价值。

Method: 测量葫芦丝的C、B、A、G、F五个音高，提取7个心理声学特征；使用COMSAR框架中的Kohonen自组织映射（SOM）对音色特征进行聚类，并定义聚类质量指数量化聚类效果。

Result: 7个特征中仅频谱质心、锐度和分形相关维数能形成音高聚类；亮度与锐度分析表明最高音不如中低音明亮和尖锐；分形相关维数（主要决定初始瞬态的混沌性）是最佳聚类特征，最高音混沌性最低。

Conclusion: 分形相关维数是葫芦丝最优的音色聚类特征，可有效区分不同音高；聚类质量指数验证了SOM聚类结果的有效性，为葫芦丝音色的心理声学建模提供了依据。

Abstract: The hulusi is a wind instrument that was invented in Yunnan Province, China, and has become tremendously popular in recent years. It consists of a mouthpiece, a gourd, and three bamboo tubes, all with free reeds made of copper. The main bamboo tube in the middle has seven finger holes. In this instrument, the pipe length, not the free reed's eigenfrequency, determines the instrument's pitch, unlike, for example, with the Western accordion or the blues harp. In this study, a machine learning model implemented in the COMSAR framework (https://github.com/ifsm) was used to investigate the timbre characteristics of the \emph{hulusi} to cluster different instruments and pitches. The measured \emph{hulusi} pitches C, B, A, G, and F were analyzed according to seven psychoacoustic features, among which only the spectral centroid, sharpness, and fractal correlation dimension are shown to form pitch clusters. These timbre features were used to train Kohonen self-organizing maps (SOMs) for clustering. Brightness and sharpness analysis revealed that the highest pitches were less bright and less sharp than mid- and low-range pitches were. Furthermore, the fractal correlation dimension, which mainly determines the chaoticity of the initial transients, was the best-clustering timbre feature for the hulusi, with the highest pitches showing the least chaoticity. This result is supported by defining a cluster quality index for the SOMs.

</details>


### [104] [The Unbearable Weight: Scaling Models and Methods for UAV Audio Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17884)
*Andrew P. Berg,Qian Zhang,Mia Y. Wang*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 论文系统比较了无人机音频分类中架构与微调方法的缩放效果，发现在数据稀缺场景下，缩放微调方法比缩放模型更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在消费与国防场景日益普及，但从有限、单一模态数据中可靠分类无人机是紧迫挑战。主流做法是大型预训练网络全量微调，计算与内存开销大，难以适应资源受限的无人机部署（需边缘推理和快速重训练）。因此需要回答：何时这种开销是合理的，何时轻量替代方案更优。

Method: 使用涵盖31类无人机、共3100个音频片段的定制数据集，评估Transformer（ViT、AST）与卷积（自定义CNN、ResNet-18/152、MobileNet-V3-S/L、EfficientNet-B0/B7）骨干，在三种微调方式下比较：全量微调、仅分类器微调、四种参数高效微调（PEFT）——SSF、IA3、OFT、选择性batch-norm微调。所有配置采用5折交叉验证，评估准确率、训练时间、可训练参数比例、推理时内存占用。

Result: EfficientNet-B7配合三倍数据增强的选择性batch-norm微调取得最高验证准确率（97.65% ± 0.30），且仅更新不到0.5%的模型参数。整个扫描中，轻量CNN在准确率和效率上均持续优于Transformer。

Conclusion: 在数据稀缺条件下的无人机音频分类中，缩放微调方法优于缩放模型，轻量CNN比Transformer更具优势，为资源受限部署提供了更高效的选择。

Abstract: As unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) become increasingly prevalent in consumer and defense settings, classifying them reliably from limited, modality-specific data is an urgent challenge. The dominant approach, large pretrained networks fully fine-tuned on task data, carries a substantial computational and memory weight that is hard to bear in resource-constrained UAV deployments, where edge inference and rapid retraining for emerging platforms are both required. This paper systematically scales across both model architectures and fine-tuning methods for UAV audio classification, asking when that weight is justified and when lighter alternatives prevail. Using a custom dataset of 3,100 audio clips spanning 31 drone classes, we evaluate transformer (ViT, AST) and convolutional (custom CNN, ResNet-18/152, MobileNet-V3-S/L, EfficientNet-B0/B7) backbones under full fine-tuning, classifier-only fine-tuning, and four parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods: SSF, IA3, OFT, and selective batch-norm tuning. All configurations are evaluated with 5-fold cross-validation across accuracy, training time, trainable-parameter share, and inference-time memory footprint. Selective batch-norm fine-tuning of EfficientNet-B7 with three-fold augmentations achieves the highest validation accuracy (97.65% +- 0.30) while updating under 0.5% of model parameters. Across the sweep, lightweight CNNs consistently outperform transformers on both accuracy and efficiency. For UAV audio classification under data scarcity, scaling the method outperforms scaling the model.

</details>


### [105] [CPR: Combining global composing, local performing and full-sequence refining in piano rendering with continuous autoregressive modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18216)
*Chong Jing,Junan Zhang,Zhizheng Wu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出 Composer--Performer--Refiner (CPR) 框架用于提示条件钢琴 MIDI-to-Music 渲染，通过连续自回归建模结合局部流匹配和波形上采样，避免了离散量化的细节损失，并引入 BREPA 与 MT-RoPE 增强语义结构与跨模态时间对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两难：离散码本自回归模型虽具因果时间建模能力，但量化会丢弃声学细节；流匹配虽能更好保留声学结构，却需全序列注意力、计算成本高且语义结构较差。作者希望兼顾条件跟随能力与分布建模能力，同时绕过量化瓶颈并降低计算成本。

Method: 提出 Composer--Performer--Refiner (CPR) 框架：Composer 自回归预测连续隐状态；Performer 通过局部流匹配生成 24kHz 声学 latent；Refiner 将波形上采样至 48kHz。并引入 Bottlenecked Representation Alignment (BREPA) 与 Modality--Time RoPE (MT-RoPE)，分别强化 Composer 隐状态中的音乐语义结构和跨模态的时间对齐。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验指标。所述方法目标为在忠实渲染目标音符的同时复现参考录音的音色，并声称相较现有范式具备更低计算成本、更好的条件跟随与分布建模能力，同时规避量化瓶颈。

Conclusion: 通过连续自回归与局部流匹配的结合，CPR 框架为提示条件钢琴 MIDI-to-Music 渲染提供了一种兼顾语义跟随、声学保真与计算效率的新范式，代码已开源。

Abstract: Prompt-conditioned piano MIDI-to-Music rendering aims to faithfully render target notes while reproducing the timbre of a reference recording. Existing approaches primarily follow two paradigms: autoregressive (AR) modeling and flow matching (or diffusion). Discrete-codec AR models provide causal temporal modeling, but quantization can discard acoustic detail. Flow matching better preserves acoustic structure in the cost of full-sequence attention costs and worse semantic structure. Continuous autoregressive models operate directly on continuous representations. It not only combines the condition-following ability of AR models and distribution-modeling capacity of flow matching but also bypasses the quantization bottleneck with lower computational costs. Building on this principle, we present Composer--Performer--Refiner (CPR) framework. Composer autoregressively predicts continuous hidden states, Performer generates 24kHz acoustic latents through local flow matching and Refiner then upsamples the waveform to 48 kHz. We further introduce Bottlenecked Representation Alignment (BREPA) and Modality--Time RoPE (MT-RoPE) to strengthen musical semantic structure in Composer hidden states and temporal alignments across modalities. Codes are available at https://github.com/FEAfeatherTHER/CPR_official

</details>


### [106] [Look Less, Hear Better: Jointly Rewarded GRPO for Streaming ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18333)
*Xiuwen Zheng*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文指出延迟流建模(DSM)中的结构延迟τ不能代表用户感知的延迟，提出词级发射延迟指标AWED，并用GRPO在联合奖励下对DSM识别器进行后训练，在单一工作点(τ=6帧)下同时优于SFT初始化和Voxtral Realtime骨干，在多个前瞻预算下改进准确率-延迟帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式ASR需同时评估转写内容与逐词提交速度。DSM是主流范式，但其结构延迟τ并非用户感知延迟的良好代理；且DSM基于对齐的监督在每个τ都使用同一强制对齐转写，迫使模型延迟提交本已可输出的词，造成延迟上的性能损失。

Method: 提出AWED——一种相对于每个词声学结束点的词级发射延迟指标；在DSM识别器上用GRPO进行后训练，奖励函数联合评估转写准确率与实测延迟；在单一工作点(τ=6帧)训练。

Result: 模型在所评估的全部前瞻预算下均优于其监督微调初始化和Voxtral Realtime骨干：在80ms结构延迟下相对降低WER 30.8%，在480ms下相对降低5.7%，同时将中位AWED从1.17s降至1.04s。

Conclusion: 以延迟为奖励的后训练无需架构改动即可推进流式ASR的准确率-延迟帕累托前沿，表明τ并非合适的延迟代理，直接优化感知延迟更为有效。

Abstract: Streaming automatic speech recognition (ASR) must be judged jointly on what it transcribes and on how quickly it commits each word. Delayed streams modeling (DSM) has become the dominant paradigm for streaming large audio-language models, exposing a structural delay $τ$ that bounds the decoder's lookahead. We show that $τ$ is a poor proxy for user-perceived latency, and that the alignment-based supervision of DSM leaves latency on the table: the same forced-aligned transcript is used at every $τ$, forcing the model to withhold words it could already commit. We introduce AWED, a word-level emission-delay metric defined relative to the acoustic end of each word, and post-train a DSM recognizer with GRPO under a reward that scores transcription accuracy and measured delay jointly. Trained at a single operating point ($τ=6$ frames), our model dominates both its supervised fine-tuning initialization and the Voxtral Realtime backbone across all evaluated lookahead budgets: it cuts WER by 30.8\% relative at an 80\,ms structural delay, and by 5.7\% relative at 480\,ms while lowering median AWED from 1.17\,s to 1.04\,s. Latency-rewarded post-training thus advances the accuracy--latency Pareto frontier of streaming ASR without architectural change.

</details>


### [107] [VoiceTrace: A Benchmark and Retrieval Framework for Who-Said-What Speech Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18521)
*Aaron Yee,Fengjie Lu,Jiarui Hai,Chenang Jiang,Helin Wang,Siwei Tu,Weitao You,Lingyun Sun*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出VoiceTrace-Bench混合语音检索基准与VoiceTrace两阶段框架，联合文本"说什么"与参考语音"谁在说"，在语义与混合检索任务上均达最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音内容在会议、讲座、播客和视频中持续增长，现有基准与模型主要关注"说了什么"（语义内容），而忽略了"是谁说的"（说话人）。真实场景中用户常需同时依据语义内容和目标说话人进行检索，且说话人往往通过一段参考语音自然指定，而非预定义身份。现有工作未能覆盖这一混合检索需求，因此存在明显空白。

Method: 1) 构建VoiceTrace-Bench基准：每条查询由指定"检索什么"的文本与指定"检索谁"的参考语音共同组成，要求模型从异构查询输入中直接整合互补的语义与说话人信息。2) 提出VoiceTrace两阶段检索框架，基于音频语言模型（ALM）的联合音文建模能力：第一阶段VoiceTrace-Emb为嵌入模型，学习统一表示以支持高效大规模检索；第二阶段VoiceTrace-Reranker为重排模型，对每个查询—候选对进行联合建模以做细粒度相关性估计。

Result: 在既有语义语音检索基准上，VoiceTrace取得state-of-the-art性能；在VoiceTrace-Bench上，其表现大幅优于基于级联（cascade）的方法。

Conclusion: VoiceTrace在传统语义检索与新的混合检索两种设定下均展现有效性，验证了显式整合语义与说话人信息的必要性与所提两阶段框架的通用性。

Abstract: Speech retrieval has become increasingly important as spoken content continues to grow across meetings, lectures, podcasts, and videos. Existing benchmarks and models have advanced semantic search over spoken content, but largely focus on \emph{what} is said while overlooking \emph{who} says it. In many real-world scenarios, however, users need to retrieve speech based jointly on semantic content and a target speaker, where the speaker may be specified naturally through a reference speech utterance rather than a predefined identity. To address this gap, we introduce \textbf{VoiceTrace-Bench}, a benchmark for hybrid speech retrieval in which each query combines text specifying \emph{what} to retrieve with reference speech specifying \emph{who} to retrieve. This setting requires models to integrate complementary semantic and speaker information directly from heterogeneous query inputs. Motivated by the joint audio-text modeling capabilities of audio-language models (ALMs), we develop \textbf{VoiceTrace}, a two-stage retrieval framework consisting of \textbf{VoiceTrace-Emb}, an embedding model that learns unified representations for efficient large-scale retrieval, and \textbf{VoiceTrace-Reranker}, a reranking model that jointly examines each query--candidate pair for fine-grained relevance estimation. Experiments show that VoiceTrace achieves state-of-the-art performance on established semantic speech retrieval benchmarks, while substantially outperforming cascade-based approaches on VoiceTrace-Bench, demonstrating its effectiveness for both conventional semantic retrieval and the new hybrid retrieval setting.

</details>


### [108] [TTM-Bench: A Framework for Text-to-Music System Performance Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18585)
*Giorgia Adorni,Michela Papandrea,Battista Rimoldi,Tiziano Leidi*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出TTM-Bench基准框架，以统一协议对文本到音乐系统进行音乐内容对齐与计算效率的双维度评测，并通过案例研究说明两维度证据互补、高对齐性并不必然对应低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到音乐（TTM）系统日益普及，但系统架构、支持的条件信息、访问方式各异，评价指标又高度碎片化、难以统一，导致可靠的性能比较困难，因此需要一套通用、可复现的评测协议。

Method: TTM-Bench定义统一评测协议，从两个维度评估：一是音乐内容对齐，通过语义、流派、音乐描述符等可解释的一致性得分对齐共同的音乐规范，并汇总为综合得分；二是计算效率，用生成延迟和实时因子刻画，对本地模型统计资源占用，对托管服务统计使用成本。

Result: 通过初步的对比案例研究验证框架有效性，结果显示两个维度捕捉到的证据具有互补性：音乐内容对齐度更高的系统并不系统地对应更低的计算开销。

Conclusion: TTM性能评估应采用彼此独立且可解释的多个度量，而非单一化的总体指标，以避免过度简化并更全面地反映系统特性。

Abstract: Text-to-music (TTM) systems are increasingly used to generate musical audio from natural-language descriptions. Robust evaluation is therefore essential, yet reliable performance comparison remains challenging. This difficulty stems from differences in system architecture, supported conditioning information, and access mode, as well as heterogeneous and fragmented metrics that cannot be applied uniformly across systems. To address these challenges, we introduce TTM-Bench, a framework that defines a common protocol for systematic, reproducible performance benchmarking of contemporary TTM systems. It evaluates performance along two dimensions: musical-content alignment, quantified by interpretable semantic, genre, and musical-descriptor agreement scores against a common musical specification and summarized by an aggregate score; and computational efficiency, characterized by generation latency and real-time factor, alongside resource use for local models and cost for hosted services. We demonstrate the framework through a preliminary comparative case study, illustrating the complementary evidence captured by these dimensions. The results show that higher musical-content alignment does not systematically coincide with lower computational demands, highlighting the importance of assessing TTM performance through distinct, interpretable measures rather than a reductive overall indicator.

</details>


### [109] [Beyond EER: Multi-Dimensional Evaluation of Information Leakage in Speaker De-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18673)
*Seungmin Seo,Oleg Aulov,P. Jonathon Phillips,Kevin Mangold,Jonathan Eskin*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文指出当前说话人去标识化(SDID)评估过度依赖EER单指标，提出包含五类互补指标的整体评估框架，并在IARPA ARTS的五个系统上证明单一指标会误判隐私特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SDID评估将隐私简化为单一维度——生物特征验证性能(通常用等错误率EER衡量)，忽视了软生物特征推断、嵌入级重识别、结构模板相似性等关键泄露通道，这些通道威胁生物参考的不可链接性(unlinkability)与不可逆性(irreversibility)，导致对系统隐私属性的评估不全面甚至误导。

Method: 提出涵盖五个互补指标的整体评估框架：(i) EER；(ii) 软生物特征泄露分数；(iii) 累积匹配特性(cumulative match characteristic)重识别分析；(iv) 典型相关分析(canonical correlation analysis)与Procrustes嵌入对齐；(v) 通过词错误率(WER)和语义相似度衡量的可懂度。并在IARPA ARTS项目的五个SDID系统上进行评估。

Result: 实验表明，这五个指标捕获了信息泄露的相互独立维度；不同系统在各指标上的表现并不一致，说明仅依赖单一指标(如EER)会歪曲SDID系统真实的隐私属性。

Conclusion: SDID隐私评估应采用多维度整体框架，而非仅依靠EER等单指标，否则难以准确反映系统在不可链接性、不可逆性及实用性方面的真实权衡。

Abstract: Speaker de-identification (SDID) aims to preserve privacy by concealing speaker identity while maintaining speech utility. However, current evaluations often reduce privacy to a single dimension - biometric verification performance - typically measured by Equal Error Rate (EER). This narrow focus ignores critical leakage channels, such as soft biometric inference, embedding-level re-identification, and structural template similarity, which threaten the unlinkability and irreversibility of biometric references. We propose a holistic evaluation framework across five complementary metrics: (i) EER, (ii) soft biometric leakage score , (iii) cumulative match characteristic re-identification analysis, (iv) canonical correlation analysis and Procrustes embedding alignment, and (v) intelligibility via word error rate and semantic similarity. Evaluating five SDID systems from the IARPA ARTS program, we demonstrate that these metrics capture independent dimensions of information leakage. Our results indicate that reliance on a single metric can misrepresent the privacy properties of an SDID system.

</details>


### [110] [Variable-Rate Harmonic-Percussive Time-Scale Modification with Real-Time Playback in Python](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18999)
*Sayema Lubis,Clark Peng,Jared Carreño,TJ Tsai*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出面向"可变速率播放"场景的谐波-打击分离时间尺度修改(TSM)方法Python实现：离线完成谐波-打击预分离，实时以逐帧可变时间尺度因子合成；并通过用预计算查找表替代相位声码器分析阶段的FFT与瞬时频率计算，提出一组近似变体，在保持感知质量的同时将运行时间降低约一半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源TSM实现几乎都面向离线处理（固定速率、预先写出结果），而自动伴奏等应用需要"可变速率播放"：待拉伸录音已知，但播放速率未知且必须随现场演奏者连续变化。少数实时实现为C++编写，偏重速度优化而非可修改性、可实验性与面向Python研究生态的集成性。因此需要一种Python实现，并解决实时性与计算开销问题。

Method: 1) 采用广泛使用的谐波-打击(HP)TSM方法，将谐波-打击分离作为对已知输入录音的离线预处理步骤；2) 合成与播放实时进行，时间尺度因子可在每一帧变化；3) 提出一族变体，将相位声码器分析阶段的FFT与瞬时频率计算替换为对预计算表的查找，以降低运行时间；4) 通过24名被试的主观听测（1114次成对评分）评估近似版本与精确版本的感知差异。

Result: 主观听测表明，当预计算帧移(hop size)足够小时，查找表近似版本与精确实现在感知上无法区分，同时总运行时间减少约一半。论文还刻画了预计算量、运行时间、内存与感知质量之间的权衡关系，并将实现以开源包形式发布。

Conclusion: 该方法为可变速率播放场景提供了易集成、易实验的Python TSM实现；用预计算查找表替代分析阶段FFT与瞬时频率计算，可在感知质量基本无损的前提下将运行时间约减半，并给出了面向算法选择的权衡指南，同时开源发布。

Abstract: Time-scale modification (TSM) has a number of open-source implementations, but these are designed almost exclusively for offline use, in which a recording is processed at a fixed rate and written out ahead of time. Applications such as automatic musical accompaniment require a different setting, which we call variable-rate playback: the recording to be stretched is known in advance, but the playback rate is not, and must change continuously in response to a live performer. The few implementations that generate output in real time are written in C++ and optimized for speed rather than for ease of modification, experimentation, and integration with the primarily Python-based research ecosystem. This paper describes a Python implementation of the widely used harmonic-percussive TSM method for the variable-rate playback setting. Harmonic-percussive separation is performed offline as a preprocessing step on the known input recording, while synthesis and playback are carried out in real time with a time-scale factor that may change at every frame. We further propose a family of variants that reduce runtime by replacing the phase vocoder's analysis-stage FFT and instantaneous frequency calculations with lookups into precomputed tables. Subjective listening tests with 24 participants (1114 pairwise ratings) show that these approximations become perceptually indistinguishable from the exact implementation once the precomputed hop size is sufficiently small, while reducing total runtime by roughly half. We characterize the resulting tradeoffs among precomputation, runtime, memory, and perceptual quality to guide algorithm selection, and we release our implementation as an open-source package.

</details>


### [111] [Multi-Teacher Distillation for Cross-Domain Streaming Electrolaryngeal Speech Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18686)
*Benedikt Mayrhofer,Enrique Orozco Olivares,Franz Pernkopf,Philipp Aichinger,Martin Hagmüller*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出多教师知识蒸馏框架，训练轻量级流式内容编码器，兼顾健康与电子喉（EL）语音，显著降低EL词错误率并实现高效CPU实时推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习（SSL）虽提升了语音表征，但在电子喉（EL）等病理语音域性能显著下降；同时SSL模型计算开销大，难以满足实时、端侧部署需求。

Method: 多教师知识蒸馏框架，渐进式蒸馏两个教师：一是冻结的SSL模型，从健康（HE）语音提供离散音素聚类目标；二是EL微调的语音识别模型，提供连续瓶颈特征目标。学生模型为轻量级流式内容编码器，并对比了因果卷积、Transformer、Conformer与Mamba四种架构。

Result: 下游语音识别评估中，EL词错误率降至21.2%，而最强零样本SSL基线为39.3%；在学生架构中，Mel-Conformer在EL准确率与计算效率间取得最佳平衡；最终编码器21.9M参数，单CPU核心ONNX Runtime下实时因子为0.30。

Conclusion: 多教师渐进式蒸馏可有效提升病理语音（EL）表征的泛化能力，同时获得轻量、流式、可端侧实时部署的内容编码器，Mel-Conformer为最优学生架构。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) has improved speech representations, yet performance degrades in pathological domains such as electrolaryngeal (EL) speech, and the computational footprint of SSL models limits their applicability in real-time, on-device deployment. We propose a multi-teacher knowledge distillation framework to train a lightweight, streaming content encoder that generalizes across healthy (HE) and EL speech. Two teachers are distilled progressively: a frozen SSL model providing discrete phonetic cluster targets from HE speech, and an EL-fine-tuned speech recognition model supplying continuous bottleneck feature targets. Evaluated via downstream speech recognition, our approach reduces the EL word error rate to 21.2%, compared to 39.3% for the strongest zero-shot SSL baseline. Among causal convolutional, Transformer, Conformer, and Mamba-based student architectures, a Mel-Conformer achieves the best combination of EL accuracy and computational efficiency. The final encoder contains 21.9,M parameters and runs at a real-time factor of 0.30 under ONNX Runtime on a single CPU core.

</details>


### [112] [TeleAntiFraud 2.0: A Refreshable, Profile-Grounded, and Audio-Based Benchmark for Telecom Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18748)
*Huiyuan Liu,Zhiming Ma,Yanxing Liu,Shun Zhang,Qifan Wang,Di Liu,Yifan Wang,Yuyang Deng,Haoyang Meng,Yijin Zhou,Yuxi Zhao,Chengxian Hu,Peidong Wang,Peng Chen*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出 TeleAntiFraud 2.0，采用混合树反诈生成流水线与月度冻结评测协议，构建含900通电话（600诈+300近域非诈）的中文音频基准，揭示近域负样本与预测崩塌是现实评测的核心要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电信诈骗话术演化快且常伪装成常规服务对话，现有基准存在两处不足：一是无法在不覆盖旧测试集的前提下纳入新诈骗模式；二是依赖主题分离的负样本，无法区分诈骗与合法近域通话，导致评测过于乐观。

Method: 提出 Mixed-Tree Anti-Fraud Generation Pipeline：将线上诈骗案例摘要转化为画像锚定场景，经混合树扩展生成，在共享上下文下同时实现诈骗与非诈骗对话路径，渲染为角色匹配语音，并对音频、标签、提示、清单与溯源记录进行按月冻结（monthly frozen evaluation protocol）。每个冻结集含900通中文通话（600诈/300近域非诈）。评测包括受控文本分类实验与整集音频、ASR+LLM 评测。

Result: 受控文本实验中，三个分类器面对无关或普通负样本时 Macro-F1 达完美（1.0），但在近域同源负样本下降至 0.65–0.68；整集音频与 ASR+LLM 评测进一步暴露类别先验捷径、预测崩塌（prediction collapse）与快照敏感性（snapshot sensitivity）。

Conclusion: 近域构造与崩塌感知报告应成为真实混淆条件下音频电信诈骗模型评测的核心要求；随附构建代码、评测脚本、清单与文档，数据集与代码已公开。

Abstract: Telecom fraud scripts evolve rapidly and are often designed to resemble routine service conversations, creating two key requirements for audio-based telecom-fraud evaluation. First, benchmarks must incorporate newly observed scam patterns without overwriting previously established test sets. Second, they must distinguish fraud from lawful, near-domain calls rather than relying on topic-separated negative examples. We present TeleAntiFraud 2.0, constructed with our Mixed-Tree Anti-Fraud Generation Pipeline and evaluated under a monthly frozen evaluation protocol. The pipeline transforms online fraud-case abstracts into profile-grounded scenarios, expands them through mixed-tree generation, realizes fraud and non-fraud dialogue paths under shared contexts, renders validated dialogues as role-matched speech, and freezes the resulting audio, labels, prompts, manifests, and provenance records for each monthly evaluation set. Each frozen set contains 900 Chinese calls, comprising 600 fraud and 300 near-domain non-fraud cases. Controlled text experiments show that three classifiers achieve perfect macro-averaged F1 (Macro-F1) when evaluated against unrelated or ordinary negatives, but drop to 0.65-0.68 with near-domain sibling negatives. Full-set audio and automatic-speech-recognition plus large-language-model (ASR+LLM) evaluations further reveal class-prior shortcuts, prediction collapse, and snapshot sensitivity. Together, these findings establish near-domain construction and collapse-aware reporting as core requirements for evaluating audio-based telecom-fraud models under realistic confusable conditions. The accompanying research artifact includes the construction code, evaluation scripts, manifests, and documentation. Our dataset and code are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/TeleAntiFraud-2_0-EEB2/.

</details>


### [113] [FRAUDSkill: Structured Frozen-Weight Skill Optimization for Audio Anti-Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18766)
*Chengxian Hu,Zhiming Ma,Mingjun Pan,Yifan Wang,Shun Zhang,Qifan Wang,Zhilei Zhao,Yijin Zhou,Yuxi Zhao,Huiyuan Liu,Peidong Wang,Peng Chen*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出FRAUDSkill，一种结构化冻结权重适配框架，通过外部技能程序、路由策略与决策规则优化音频反诈检测，而不改动底层音频语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型音频语言模型在反诈检测上有潜力，但部署要求预测遵循预定义标签空间与结构化决策协议（服务场景识别、欺诈检测、条件式欺诈类型分类）。现有微调与提示方法把任务知识、约束和决策规则编码进模型参数或手工维护的提示中，难以随欺诈模式与标注政策演进而适配。

Method: FRAUDSkill保持底层音频语言模型冻结不变，优化外部一层的技能程序、路由特定策略与决策规则；并结合结构化输出控制与验证引导的多路径推理，保证输出符合协议。

Result: 在TeleAntiFraud基准上达到73.50% Macro-F1，比共享冻结模型基线高出31.96%，同时将无效输出降至1.94%。

Conclusion: 外部技能优化为结构化音频反诈检测提供了有效且可适配的方案，无需修改底层模型。

Abstract: Large audio-language models have shown promise for anti-fraud detection by directly processing speech and reasoning over fraud-related evidence. Their deployment, however, requires predictions to follow a predefined label space and a structured decision protocol consisting of service-scenario identification, fraud detection, and conditional fraud-type classification. Existing fine-tuning and prompt-based approaches typically encode task knowledge, constraints, and decision rules into model parameters or manually maintained prompts, making them difficult to adapt as fraud patterns and labeling policies evolve. To this end, we propose FRAUDSkill, a structured frozen-weight adaptation framework that leaves the underlying audio-language model unchanged while optimizing an external layer of skill programs, route-specific policies, and decision rules. We further combine structured output control with validation-guided multi-path inference to ensure protocol-compliant predictions. On the TeleAntiFraud benchmark, FRAUDSkill achieves 73.50% Macro-F1, outperforming the shared frozen-model baseline by 31.96% while reducing invalid outputs to 1.94%. Extensive experiments demonstrate that external skill optimization provides an effective and adaptable solution for structured audio anti-fraud detection without modifying the underlying model. The source code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/FRAUDSKILL-114514.

</details>


### [114] [What Affects the Performance of Fake Audio Detection? Analyzing Factors in a Continual Learning Setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19067)
*Yixuan Xiao,Ngoc Thang Vu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文在持续学习场景下系统考察攻击者架构、攻击者训练数据集、说话人多样性及任务顺序对伪造音频检测系统性能的影响，并对比直接微调、单类分类、随机回放与LwF四种训练策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度伪造音频生成技术日益精进，检测系统需要具备随时间持续适应的能力，但目前对持续学习场景下影响检测性能的关键因素缺乏系统认识。

Method: 在持续学习设定下，围绕攻击者架构、攻击者训练数据集、说话人多样性和任务顺序四类因素，采用直接微调、单类分类、随机回放和Learning without Forgetting四种策略训练三个检测模型，并比较其性能。

Result: 伪造音频中的伪影可能源自攻击者的训练数据集，仅改变攻击者架构不足以有效挑战检测系统；任务顺序与说话人多样性会显著影响性能，且不同检测模型和训练策略的敏感程度存在差异。

Conclusion: 开发鲁棒的伪造音频检测系统时，需审慎考虑攻击者数据来源、说话人多样性及任务顺序等因素，并针对不同模型与策略的敏感性差异进行设计。

Abstract: The increasing sophistication of deepfake audio generation technologies makes it important to develop robust fake audio detection systems that can adapt over time. This study examines how various factors impact the performance of detection systems in a continual learning setting. We focus on factors such as attacker architectures, attackers' training datasets, speaker diversity, and task order. We evaluate the performance of three detection models trained with four different strategies, including direct fine-tuning, one-class classification, random replay, and Learning without Forgetting. Results show that artifacts from the fake audios might arise from the attackers' training datasets, and simply changing attacker architectures does not sufficiently challenge detection systems. Moreover, task order and speaker diversity can significantly influence performance, with varying degrees of sensitivity across different detection models and training strategies. These insights underline the need for careful consideration of these factors when developing robust detection systems.

</details>


<div id='nlin.PS'></div>

# nlin.PS [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Two-Parameter Family of Nonlinear Dirac Equations With Scalar-Scalar plus Vector-Vector Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18170)
*Avinash Khare,Fred Cooper,John F. Dawson,Avadh Saxena*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 获得1+1维非线性Dirac方程的精确孤波解，研究其电荷、能量、E/Q比值、电荷密度形态及稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究标量-标量加矢量-矢量相互作用下非线性Dirac方程的孤波解，探索束缚态存在的参数空间及其性质。

Method: 求解形式为ψ(x,t)=e^{-iωt}ψ(x)的精确解，计算电荷Q与能量E，分析(p,κ)参数空间，并导出非相对论修正非线性Schrödinger方程(NLSE)讨论稳定性。

Result: 对所有0<ω<m均找到解；E/Q独立于耦合常数g；pκ≤1时电荷密度为单峰，pκ>1时由双峰过渡为单峰；所有p下E/Q在κ=2处出现转变，推测与不稳定性起始相关。

Conclusion: 非线性Dirac方程存在丰富的孤波束缚态解，其性质依赖于参数p、κ与ω，κ=2可能是稳定性转变的临界点。

Abstract: We obtain exact solutions of the nonlinear Dirac equation in $1+1$ dimensions of the form $ψ(x,t) = e^{-iωt} ψ(x)$ for scalar-scalar (SS) plus vector-vector (VV) interaction with interaction Lagrangian given by $$L_{I} = \frac{g^2}{κ+1}[(\barψ ψ)^{κ+1} +\frac{1}{p} (\barψ γ_μψ\barψ γ^μ ψ)^{κ+1}]$$ where $p > 0$ but arbitrary otherwise. We look for solutions with $0 < ω< m$ where $ω, m$ are frequency and mass, respectively. We find solutions for all values of $ω$ in this range. We compute the charge $Q$ and the energy $E$ for each of the solitary wave solutions and explore the region in the ($p, κ$) parameter space in which solitary wave bound states exist (i.e., for which $E/Q < m$). We show that for all the cases while both $E$ and $Q$ depend on the coupling constant $g$, their ratio $E/Q$ is independent of $g$. We further find that in case $pκ\le 1$, the charge density for all the solitary waves have only single hump while for $p κ> 1$ there is a transition from double to single hump and we determine it as a function of $ω/m$. We notice that for all $p$ there is a transition at $κ=2$ in the behavior of $E/Q$ as a function of $ω$ which we speculate is related to the onset of instability of the solutions at $κ=2$. We obtain the nonrelativistic reduction of the two-parameter family to a non-relativistic modified nonlinear Schrödinger equation (NLSE) and discuss the stability of the single hump olitary waves in the domain of validity of the modified NLSE.

</details>
